1b:["$","ul","ul-3",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":["2026-07-25 — ",["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"⚠️ The correlated-blind-spot rail reaches the half of the loop that CHOOSES the experiment, and\narrives with a measurement."}]," Bisht et al. 2026 (arXiv 2605.08956, a ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"position paper"}],") posed 50 materials-science\npapers to ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"six frontier models from two independent providers"}]," (three Anthropic, three OpenAI) on two tasks: one\nwith a determinate answer, one asking for ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"novel"}]," hypotheses. Their conclusion: ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":["\"A research community that queries multiple AI systems for novel hypotheses is, from an\nepistemic standpoint, ",["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"effectively sampling from a single model"}],",\""]}]," and ",["$","em","em-2",{"children":"\"the effective epistemic sample size …\nis close to one regardless of how many systems are consulted.\""}]," ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"This is the vault's rail one layer up."}]," Every\none of the vault's eight blind-spot rows is two ",["$","em","em-3",{"children":"assays"}]," sharing a structural gap; this is ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"N models sharing a\ntraining corpus and convergent preference optimisation"}]," — different companies, different weights, the same\nupstream cause of the same narrowing. ",["$","strong","strong-6",{"children":"And the vault's existing remedy applies unchanged: the fix for a\ncorrelated blind spot is a measurement that fails differently, not a bigger battery — so consulting a second\nmodel is a bigger battery."}]," Two further arguments land on the vault's own gaps: ",["$","strong","strong-7",{"children":"problem selection"}]," drifts\ntoward quantifiable, data-rich questions (the McNamara fallacy; cited measurement — AI adoption ",["$","em","em-4",{"children":"shrinks the\ncollective volume of topics studied by 4.63%"}]," while its users publish 3.02× more), which in this vault means a\nloop will work the plate-reader endpoints and structurally avoid the ones with ",["$","strong","strong-8",{"children":"no method at all"}],"; and\n",["$","strong","strong-9",{"children":"benchmarks"}]," score single-turn prediction with no path from a physical result back to the model, so ",["$","strong","strong-10",{"children":"nothing\nin the field measures the one operation that makes a loop a loop."}]," ",["$","code","code-0",{"children":"strength: suggestive"}]," — not peer-reviewed,\nsix models, one embedding model, one domain, and cosine similarity is a ",["$","em","em-5",{"children":"proxy"}]," for epistemic independence. 1d:["$","ul","ul-4",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"⚠️ THE INVENTORY GAINS A SIXTH PART, UNDERNEATH ALL THE OTHERS: CAN THE ROBOT SEE THE OBJECT?"}]," This map's\nrunning inventory is ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"mind"}]," (Robin: hypotheses), ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"hands"}]," (",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/nams/themes/automation-tox21-qhts-platform","children":"automation-tox21-qhts-platform"}],"),\n",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"wire"}]," (LAP), ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"ledger"}]," (ToxMCP), ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"guard"}]," (AEGIS), all seen running once (ProtoPilot). ",["$","strong","strong-8",{"children":["Two consequences: the\ncorrelation is not evidence (it is a second, independent reason the same methods are easy), and the physical\nrung is ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"shaped"}]," rather than uniform."]}]," The methods needing a flask, a tube, a perfusion reservoir or a\nbioreactor — ",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/nams/themes/hepatotoxicity-liver-chip-mps","children":"hepatotoxicity-liver-chip-mps"}],", spheroid and organoid work, anything at volume — sit in\nthe worst part of the curve, and they are also the methods with the strongest claim to human physiological\nrelevance. 1e:["$","h3","h3-1",{"children":"Added 2026-08-02 (the measuring stick gets measured; and the dial that makes it think harder makes it see things)"}]
1f:["$","ul","ul-5",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":["⚠️ THE EVIDENCE BASE OF THIS ENTIRE MAP GETS AUDITED, AND EVERY BENCHMARK EXAMINED HAD FLAWS. Status HELD\nat ",["$","code","code-0",{"children":"emerging"}],"; nothing moves, and that is the point — what moves is how the numbers already here should be\nread."]}]," Ingested mohl-2026-agentic-benchmark-validity-scanners (arXiv 2607.27518, submitted 29\nJuly 2026, ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"preprint, not peer reviewed"}],"; UK AI Security Institute + Generality Labs + MARS + independents;\nno funding statement, recorded as missing information). Nine authors built LLM \"scanners\" for four classes of\nbenchmark validity flaw — ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"ground truth access"}]," (the answer is reachable in the environment), ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"tool\nfailure"}]," (the harness broke, not the model), ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"guessing vulnerability"}],", ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"answer format ambiguity"}]," — wrote\nhuman grading rubrics for each, and ran them over five widely used agentic benchmarks. ",["$","strong","strong-6",{"children":"Verified issues in\nall five: SWE-Bench-Verified, CORE-Bench, KernelBench, CVE-Bench, Terminal-Bench-2.0."}]," CORE-Bench hit all\nfour classes. ",["$","strong","strong-7",{"children":["SWE-Bench-",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"Verified"}]," — the name records that somebody already hand-checked it — still leaked\nground truth through overly descriptive prompts and through the agent's access to upstream git history."]}],"\n",["$","strong","strong-8",{"children":"⚠ Why this lands on this map rather than passing by:"}]," virtually every automation claim the vault holds is\na benchmark number — ProtoPilot's 88.24% instrument pass rate, Pipette's 37–72%, LabBench2's 1,912 tasks,\nLabRobFail's 90.83%→71.02%, AEGIS's F1 0.97/0.71. ",["$","strong","strong-9",{"children":"The vault has been discounting those for who produced\nthem; this says to discount them for how they were built"}],", and manual verification is demonstrably not\nsufficient protection. ",["$","strong","strong-10",{"children":"No inflation magnitude is measured anywhere"}]," — \"flaws exist in all five\" is not \"the\nscores are N points high,\" and reading a size into it would be the same over-claim the paper is about. ",["$","strong","strong-11",{"children":"No\nlaboratory benchmark was audited and no toxicology appears; the transfer is this vault's inference, labelled."}],"\n→ ",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/nams/concepts/every-agentic-benchmark-audited-had-validity-flaws","children":"every-agentic-benchmark-audited-had-validity-flaws"}]]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-1",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"⚠️ AND THE AUDITOR IS ITSELF A POOR INSTRUMENT — THE RAIL, ARRIVING AT THE QUALITY-ASSURANCE LAYER."}]," The\nsame paper measures its own scanners against human labels, and the agreement collapses depending on what is\nbeing looked for and which model is looking: ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"F1 0.74"}]," at best (guessing, GPT-5.4) down to ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"F1 0.14"}]," at\nworst (tool failure, Sonnet 4.6), with ground-truth-access at 0.50 for both and answer-format ambiguity at\n0.30/0.37. Specificity looks excellent throughout (0.84–0.97) and is misleading, because the flaws are rare:\nmost positive calls are false alarms. ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"The proposed fix for \"we cannot trust the measurement\" is another\nlanguage-model measurement"}]," — the vault's correlated-blind-spot rail landing not between two assays and not\nbetween two hypothesis-generators, but ","$L59",", which is where the whole\nagentic-AI field's quality story runs. ","$L5a"," the two models ","$L5b"," (sensitivity 0.74 vs 0.17 on tool failure; 0.93 vs\n0.44 on guessing), which is evidence against the strong reading of\n","$L5c",". Models appear to converge on what they ","$L5d","\nand diverge on what they ","$L5e"," — two different operations, and the vault should carry both rather than\ngeneralising one to the other. ","$L5f",", after AEGIS (F1 0.47 at the smallest pipette volumes) and\nLabRobFail (ground-truth labels written by GPT-5.4).\n→ ","$L60"]}],"\n"]}],"\n","$L61","\n","$L62","\n","$L63","\n","$L64","\n","$L65","\n","$L66","\n","$L67","\n"]}]

2a:["$","h3","h3-4",{"children":"Added 2026-07-19 (LAP — the agent-to-instrument protocol)"}]

38:["$","ul","ul-16",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"⚠️ THE DECISION LEG NOW HAS THREE MEASUREMENTS AND THEY DISAGREE ABOUT WHAT TO MEASURE."}]," The asymmetry\nrecorded on 2026-07-31 — execution measured twice, decision measured never — closed on 2026-08-03 with two\n",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"outcome"}]," numbers (a simulated trajectory benchmark, and a physical 24-experiment campaign in which a blind\nlanguage model placed fourth of four behind deterministic grid search). ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"A loop\nscored on outcomes cannot see this"}],", and a loop chooses its next experiment from the route, not from the\nfinal answer."]}],"\n",["$","li","li-1",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"⚠️ AND THE PROTOCOL COUNT UNDERCUTS A SHORTCUT THIS VAULT HAS BEEN LEANING ON."}]," Since 2026-08-03 this\nmap has carried the observation that an OECD Test Guideline ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"is"}]," a fixed sequence of physical operations\nwith specified parameters and ranges, so a reviewed, typed, range-checked action list is close to a direct\nencoding of guideline compliance. ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"Tonight puts a number against the gap in that reasoning"}],": written\nprotocols leave roughly a dozen state-dependent watch-for conditions and forty physical steps per document\nunstated, and the best available parser recovers the watch-fors at about F1 0.89. And\nfewest errors is not safest: ",["$","strong","strong-8",{"children":"Qwen3.6-Plus posted the lowest raw hallucination rate on the pathology\nsuite — 0.009, under one bad step in a hundred — and one of the highest weighted severities, 0.432"}],",\nbecause its rare errors landed on the concepts everything else depends on. Authors' conclusion: ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"\"terminal accuracy alone is an insufficient signal\nof agent reliability.\""}]," ",["$","strong","strong-9",{"children":"This breaks the obvious deployment design"}]," — score on a validation set, deploy\nabove a bar — because in a loop the intermediate steps are what the next experiment is chosen from, and a\nright answer reached wrongly still picks the wrong next experiment.\n→ ",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/nams/concepts/a-right-answer-is-not-evidence-of-right-reasoning","children":"a-right-answer-is-not-evidence-of-right-reasoning"}],"\n",["$","strong","strong-10",{"children":"(3) ⚠ THE CORRELATED-BLIND-SPOT RAIL, ARRIVING AT THE KNOWLEDGE LAYER — WITH A DISTRIBUTION."}]," Of 141\nfailure trajectories, ~33% were workflow breakdowns and ",["$","strong","strong-11",{"children":"67% traced to flawed domain knowledge"}]," — and\nthose were sharply non-uniform: ",["$","strong","strong-12",{"children":"one concept cluster accounted for nearly half of them"}],", with a long tail\ncontributing under five cases each. The authors call it ",["$","em","em-2",{"children":"\"systematic blind spots tied to specific\nhigh-traffic concepts.\""}]," ","$L214"," The vault's\nremedy carries over unchanged: the fix is a measurement that fails differently, so consulting the agent\ntwice is a bigger battery, not a second vote. ","$L215",", after assay↔assay,\nmodel↔model, tool↔tool-inside-a-vote, and benchmark↔its-own-audit.\n→ ","$L216","\n","$L217"," Counterfactually correcting one hallucinated step,\nfreezing everything upstream and re-running fixed ","$L218"," — but ","$L219"," when the\nagent had the right evidence and connected it badly, against ","$L21a"," when it started from a false fact:\n","$L21b","\nBad logic is recoverable; a bad premise is about a third recoverable.\n","$L21c"," The hallucination labels are produced by\nvalidator and planner agents with ","$L21d"," That is the ","$L21e"," checking layer on this map with that property, after LabRobFail's\nGPT-5.4-written ground truth, the benchmark-validity scanners at F1 0.14–0.74 against humans, and AEGIS's\nvision-model votes. Add ","$L21f"," as a standing\ndiscount on a builder-scored benchmark.\n","$L220"," Protein biology and clinical pathology\nquestion-answering.
